In particular, the application to large-scale industrial simulations confirms its suitability for aerospace certification workflows and highlights its relevance for real world engineering environments.

</details>


### [113] [Scaling Demand-Side Flexibility Through Dynamic Tariffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13401)
*Lucas Brylle,Niels Andersen,Henrik Madsen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文论证了通过动态电价激励的隐性需求侧灵活性是解决丹麦配电网电气化与可再生能源整合所面临过载、电压不稳及基础设施投资等挑战的最具可扩展性和成本效益的方案，可延迟或避免电网加固，每个受限变电站节省1300-4800万丹麦克朗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丹麦配电网在电气化和可再生能源整合过程中面临备用容量不足、元件过载退化、电压不稳定以及基础设施投资需求增长等严峻运营挑战，亟需可扩展且经济高效的解决方案。

Method: 基于实证消费数据（展示强价格响应行为）、丹麦市场模型3.0和电价模型3.0等监管框架下的价格变动分析，以及经济分析，对比显性灵活性机制与隐性需求侧灵活性机制的可扩展性和成本效益。

Result: 显性灵活性机制虽提供运营确定性，但无法跨异构客户群体扩展以解决系统级拥堵；隐性DSF机制可通过动态电价将实时电网约束传递给消费者，每个受限变电站可节省1300-4800万丹麦克朗，并带来避免峰值发电成本、减少连接延迟、降低停电风险等额外价值。

Conclusion: 隐性需求侧灵活性机制是实现收入中性的可扩展灵活性解决方案的必要路径，能延迟或避免昂贵的电网加固，同时维持系统可靠性；动态电价是实时传达电网容量约束的关键机制。

Abstract: The ongoing electrification and integration of renewable energy sources in Denmark's distribution grids pose significant operational challenges, including insufficient reserve capacity, component degradation due to overload, voltage instability, and increasing infrastructure investment requirements. This article argues that implicit demand-side flexibility (DSF) incentivized through dynamic tariffs offers the most scalable and cost-effective approach to address these challenges in a modern distribution network. We demonstrate that while explicit flexibility mechanisms provide operational certainty, they cannot scale to address system-wide congestion across heterogeneous customer bases. Drawing on empirical consumption data showing strong price-responsive behavior, varying prices due to, e.g., regulatory frameworks including the Danish Market Model 3.0 and Tariff Model 3.0, and economic analysis, we demonstrate potential grid savings of 13--48 million DKK per constrained substation through deferred or avoided reinforcement. We argue that implicit DSF mechanisms represent the necessary pathway for revenue-neutral scalable flexibility solutions that can defer costly grid reinforcements while maintaining system reliability. Beyond direct grid savings, additional value streams include avoided peak generation costs, reduced connection delays, and lower outage risk, further strengthening the economic case. Critically, dynamic tariffs offer the mechanism through which real-time grid constraints can be communicated to consumers, enabling price signals that accurately reflect the actual state of the capacity of the distribution network at any given point in time and space.

</details>

### [114] [Lower bounds on expressions depending on the functions {\boldmath$\varphi(n)$}, {\boldmath$ψ(n)$} and {\boldmath$σ(n)$}, III](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12484)
*S. I. Dimitrov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了与算术函数 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 相关的各种表达式的下界，并建立了若干显式估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究算术函数 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 相关表达式的下界，以填补现有估计的空白或改进已知结果。

Method: 通过解析数论或初等方法，对涉及 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 的表达式进行下界推导，建立显式估计式。

Result: 建立了若干关于 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 相关表达式的显式下界估计。

Conclusion: 本文为这些算术函数的下界提供了明确的量化结果，有助于数论中相关问题的进一步研究。

Abstract: This work is concerned with the study of lower bounds for various expressions related to the arithmetic functions $\varphi(n)$, $ψ(n)$ and $σ(n)$. Several explicit estimates are established.

</details>


### [115] [Root Clusters and Multiclusters over Imperfect Hilbertian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12660)
*Shubham Jaiswal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将根簇理论从完美域推广到任意域（不一定是完美域），引入了根簇大小、多簇大小、根容量、多根容量、升序指标、升序正规指标、交集指标、交集正规指标、复合指标和复合正规指标等概念，并在希尔伯特域上建立了这些广义概念的逆问题结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将根簇理论从完美域推广到一般的非完美域，并研究相关逆问题。

Method: 定义了根簇大小、多簇大小、根容量、多根容量、升序指标、升序正规指标、交集指标、交集正规指标、复合指标和复合正规指标等一系列概念，并在希尔伯特域上研究这些概念的逆问题。

Result: 在希尔伯特域上证明了：对于给定的次数、簇大小和多簇大小，存在相应的多项式；对于给定的根容量和多根容量，存在相应的域扩张。

Conclusion: 将先前在数域上的结果推广到了更一般的希尔伯特域上。

Abstract: We extend the theory of root clusters from perfect fields to general fields which are not necessarily perfect. We introduce the following notions for field extensions over any given base field and study their interesting properties: root cluster size, multicluster size and their generalizations root capacity, multiroot capacity; ascending index, ascending normal index and their generalizations intersection indicium, intersection normal indicium; compositum indicium and compositum normal indicium. We establish our results on the Inverse problems for these generalized notions over Hilbertian fields which generalizes our earlier results which were over number fields. In particular, we show over a given Hilbertian field, the existence of a polynomial for given degree, cluster size and multicluster size and existence of an extension for given root capacity and multiroot capacity with respect to that polynomial.

</details>


### [116] [An AI Proof of 18-Variable Undecidability for Diophantine Equations over $\mathbb Z[i]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12776)
*Yuchen Ding,Junfeng Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用人工智能提出了一种证明：在Gaussian整数环上，18个未知数的多项式方程是否存在解是不可判定的。该结果改进了Matiyasevich和Sun的20未知数定理，通过省略消分母变量和吸收非零条件，节省了两个变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Matiyasevich和Sun关于Gaussian整数上多项式方程可解性不可判定定理的未知数个数，将下限从20降低到18，从而更接近理论最优值。

Method: 1) 遵循Matiyasevich和Sun的有理性准则和整数检验框架；2) 通过施加两个整数条件，避免了消分母变量；3) 利用单变量技巧$(2R+1)(3R+1)$，将剩余的非零条件吸收到关系组合引理中，从而节省了两个变量。

Result: 将Gaussian整数上多项式方程可解性不可判定定理的未知数个数从20降低到18。

Conclusion: 存在一个关于Gaussian整数的18个未知数的多项式方程，其解的存在性问题是算法不可判定的。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI proof that there is no algorithm deciding whether a polynomial equation over the Gaussian integers in $18$ unknowns has a solution. The proof improves the $20$-unknown theorem of Matiyasevich and Sun. It follows their rationality criterion and integer test, but saves two variables: the clearing-denominator variable is avoided by imposing two integer conditions, and the remaining nonzero condition is absorbed into the relation-combining lemma by the one-variable gadget $(2R+1)(3R+1)$.

</details>


### [117] [Splitting of Polynomial Families via Galois Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12810)
*Tianhao Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过经典Galois论和Chebotarev密度定理，在仿射正规簇上研究有限域上多项式族的分裂行为，推广了Slavov关于几个多项式取平方值独立性的结果到n次幂剩余情形，并将其重新表述为Kummer扩张的互素线性无关条件，最后简述了到étale基本群的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将算术几何中广为流传但鲜有明确书写的技术（参数化多项式族在有限域上的分裂行为）系统化整理，并推广Slavov关于平方值独立性的结果到n次幂剩余情形。

Method: 基于经典Galois理论和仿射正规簇上的Chebotarev密度定理，避免使用Grothendieck étale拓扑的繁重工具；利用Kummer扩张的互素线性无关条件重新表述独立性条件。

Result: 当q ≡ 1 (mod n)时，将Slavov的平方值独立性结果推广到n次幂剩余情形，并将独立性条件重新解释为Kummer扩张互素线性无关的自然条件。

Conclusion: 建立了经典方法与现代étale基本群语言之间的桥梁，将底空间推广到F_q上有限型几何整正规概形。

Abstract: We study the splitting behavior of parametrized families of polynomials over finite fields through a geometric and Galois-theoretic approach. While the underlying techniques are widely considered folklore in arithmetic geometry, they have rarely been written down explicitly. To maximize accessibility, we develop a framework based on classical Galois theory and the Chebotarev Density Theorem over an affine normal variety, avoiding the heavy machinery of Grothendieck's étale topology.
  The primary goal is to extend and conceptually explain a recent result by Slavov, which established the condition for square values of several polynomials over a finite field to be independent. In the case where $q\equiv 1 \pmod n$, we generalize this phenomenon to $n$-th power residues, and reframe this independence condition as the natural condition on Kummer extensions to be mutually linearly disjoint. Finally, we briefly mention how these results can be translated into the modern language of étale fundamental groups, generalizing the base to geometrically integral, normal schemes of finite type over $\mathbb{F}_q$.

</details>


### [118] [Effective Estimates for a Class of Farey Fraction Sums and Bounds for Mundici-Type Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12907)
*Anji Dong,Huy Xuan Nguyen,Vi Anh Nguyen,Alexandru Zaharescu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Mundici猜想从完整区间(0,1]上的连续间距推广到子区间和h-间距情形，并建立了相应的Mundici型上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Daniele Mundici关于Farey序列平方间距和的猜想，将其从完整区间和连续间距的情形扩展到更一般的子区间和h-间距设定下。

Method: 通过分析Farey序列在子区间上的分布性质以及h-间距的统计特性，对D₂(Q)的推广量进行渐近估计，并利用已知结果《DLN2026》中的方法进行扩展。

Result: 在子区间和h-间距两种推广设定下，均得到了与Mundici猜想类似的上界（小于3），从而验证了猜想在更广泛框架下的成立性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Mundici猜想从原始的特殊情形推广至两个一般化方向，扩展了该猜想在Farey序列间距分析中的适用范围。

Abstract: Let $D_{2}(Q)$ denote the sum of squared distances between consecutive Farey fractions in the full interval $(0, 1]$. Daniele Mundici conjectured that $C(Q):=D_{2}(Q)\cdot Q^2/\log Q$ is less than 3 for all $Q\geq 2$, which is confirmed true in \cite{DLN2026}. In this paper, we generalize this result to subintervals of $(0, 1]$ and to $h$-spacings. As applications, we obtain Mundici-type bounds in these two settings, extending the full-interval consecutive-spacing case of Mundici's conjecture.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-target hyperbolic sieves and elliptic trace obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13018)
*Pantelimon Stănică,Erik Mulder,Markus Hittmeir*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了双素数的双曲筛集，给出了其精确计数公式、并集大小与椭圆曲线Frobenius迹的关联，以及多目标估计和幂约束版本的完整局部分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为确定性整数分解算法提供局部筛法的理论支撑，特别是针对两个目标（原根情形）的同步筛分问题，需要精确的局部计数而非渐进估计。

Method: 利用Legendre符号的四次特征和将筛集并集大小转化为椭圆曲线$Y^2=(X^2-A)(X^2-B)$的Frobenius迹，借助Hasse-Weil界得到$O(\sqrt\ell)$误差；通过组合论证和初等对合论证分别处理多目标情形和幂约束情形。

Result: (1) 证明了$|H_a(N;\ell)\cup H_b(N;\ell)|$的精确公式用四次特征和$K(A,B)$表示；(2) 该并集大小等于$3\ell/4$加椭圆曲线Frobenius迹（符号和常数调整），Hasse-Weil给出$O(\sqrt\ell)$误差，负迹解释反例；(3) 多目标估计：$k$个不同系数时并集大小与$\ell(1-2^{-k})$的偏差有$(k-1+2^{-k})\sqrt\ell+k$上界；(4) 特殊输入$N=u^rv$时幂约束筛集$H_{a,r}(N;\ell)$的精确大小通过对合论证确定。

Conclusion: 该工作将局部筛问题转化为有限域上的显式陈述，并通过验证性局部测试确认了理论结果的有效性，为确定性整数分解算法的筛步骤提供了严格的局部分析框架。

Abstract: Let $N=pq$ be a semiprime and let $\ell\nmid Na$ be an odd prime. The hyperbolic sieve set $H_a(N;\ell)=\{ax+Nx^{-1}:x\in\mathbb F_\ell^*\}$ contains the residue of the linear form $ap+q$ modulo $\ell$ and has exact cardinality $(\ell+χ(aN))/2$, where $χ$ is the Legendre symbol modulo $\ell$. We study simultaneous sieving for several linear forms and give a complete local analysis of the two-target primitive-root case proposed in connection with deterministic integer factorization. For two distinct coefficients $a,b$, with $A=4aN$ and $B=4bN$, we prove an exact formula for $|H_a(N;\ell)\cup H_b(N;\ell)|$ in terms of the degree-four character sum \[ K(A,B)=\sum_{z\in\mathbb F_\ell}χ((z^2-A)(z^2-B)).\] For a smooth projective genus-one curve $E/\mathbb{F}_\ell$, we write $t_E=\ell+1-\#E(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$ for its Frobenius trace. With this convention, $K(A,B)$ is the Frobenius trace, up to sign and an additive constant, of the genus-one curve $Y^2=(X^2-A)(X^2-B)$. Hence Hasse--Weil gives a uniform $O(\sqrt\ell)$ error from the main term $3\ell/4$, and negative traces explain the counterexamples to the pointwise bound $3\ell/4+1$. We also prove a multi-target estimate \[\left|\left|\bigcup_{j=1}^k H_{a_j}(N;\ell)\right|-\ell(1-2^{-k})\right|\le (k-1+2^{-k})\sqrt\ell+k\] for distinct coefficients $a_1,\ldots,a_k$, together with the corresponding CRT product bound. Finally, for special-shape inputs $N=u^rv$, we study the $r$-power-constrained image $H_{a,r}(N;\ell)$ and determine its exact size by an elementary involution argument. These results recast the proposed local sieve questions as explicit finite-field statements with verified local tests.

</details>


### [120] [Ramanujan's and Lim's Identities and Harmonic Maass--Jacobi Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13173)
*Kathrin Bringmann,Rajat Gupta,Badri Vishal Pandey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lim对Ramanujan奇zeta值恒等式的扩展，引入了Eisenstein级数的Eichler积分的Jacobi类比，构造了负权重的显式完备化，证明了它们是（奇异）调和Maass-Jacobi形式，描述了其非全纯部分、Ramanujan型反演公式，并研究了它们在Maass升降算子及挠点下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Ramanujan关于奇zeta值的经典恒等式推广到更广泛的框架中，并建立Eisenstein级数的Eichler积分在Jacobi形式语境下的类比，以探索模形式与zeta值之间更深层的联系。

Method: 通过构造负权重下的显式完备化，将这些对象嵌入模框架，证明它们满足（奇异）调和Maass-Jacobi形式的结构；利用Eichler积分描述其非全纯部分，建立Ramanujan型反演公式，并研究Maass升降算子及挠点作用下的性质。

Result: 成功构造了Jacobi型的Eichler积分，建立了其调和Maass-Jacobi形式身份，推导了非全纯部分的显式表达及Ramanujan型反演公式，刻画了Maass算子与挠点作用下的行为。

Conclusion: 该项工作拓展了Ramanujan恒等式在模形式领域的应用边界，为调和Maass-Jacobi形式理论提供了新的构造与实例，并揭示了其与Eichler积分及特殊zeta值之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We study an extension of Ramanujan's identities for odd zeta values by Lim and introduce Jacobi analogues of classical Eichler integrals of Eisenstein series. In negative weight we construct explicit completions and embed these objects into a modular framework by showing that they are (singular) harmonic Maass--Jacobi forms. We further describe their non-holomorphic parts in terms of Eichler integrals, establish Ramanujan-type inversion formulas, and study their behavior under the Maass raising and lowering operators and at torsion points.

</details>


### [121] [Discrete Fourier Transform Approach to Cyclically Covering Subspaces of $\mathbb{F}^n_q$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13307)
*Yangcheng Li,Pingzhi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域F_q^n上循环覆盖子空间的最大余维数h_q(n)，通过离散傅里叶变换给出了gcd(q,n)=1时h_q(n)=0的充要条件，并建立了与循环码中全重码字的等价关系。在q和n为素数且n>q、q为模n原根的情形下给出了h_q(n)的统一刻画，证明了h_2(n)≥2，h_q(n)=0（q≠2）。同时给出了h_3(n)≥1的条件，并在广义黎曼假设下证明了存在无穷多个素数n使得h_q(n)=0。此外还得到了h_q(mn)的不等式、通过伽罗瓦下降得到h_{q^m}(n)≤h_q(n)等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环覆盖子空间在编码理论和组合数学中具有重要应用，研究者希望通过确定最大可能余维数h_q(n)来理解向量空间在循环移位下的覆盖性质，并建立与循环码中全重码字存在性的深层联系。

Method: 使用离散傅里叶变换（DFT）分析gcd(q,n)=1情形，推导h_q(n)=0的充要条件；利用原根理论和数论方法刻画素数情形；通过伽罗瓦下降建立不同扩域间的关系；采用代数组合构造达到上界⌊log_q(n)⌋；结合广义黎曼假设获得平均下界估计。

Result: 给出了h_q(n)=0的充要条件及其与循环码全重码字的等价性；完全刻画了q和n为素数且q为模n原根时的h_q(n)值；证明了h_3(n)≥1的条件；在GRH下得到无穷多素数使h_q(n)=0；建立了h_q(mn)的两种下界不等式以及h_{q^m}(n)≤h_q(n)的关系；推广了达到上界⌊log_q(n)⌋的构造类；在GRH下给出了q=2,3时h_q(n)的平均下界。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了循环覆盖子空间理论的代数框架，通过离散傅里叶变换、原根理论和伽罗瓦下降等工具，完整刻画了多种情形下h_q(n)的行为，并与循环码理论建立了深刻联系。广义黎曼假设下的结果揭示了该问题的数论深度。

Abstract: Let $q$ be a prime power and $n$ a positive integer. A subspace \( U \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^n \) is called cyclically covering if the union of all its cyclic shifts covers the whole space \( \mathbb{F}_q^n \). Let \( h_q(n) \) denote the maximum possible codimension of such a subspace. When \(\gcd(q,n)=1\), we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for \(h_q(n)=0\) via Discrete Fourier Transforms, and prove this equality is equivalent to the existence of full-weight codewords in cyclic codes of \(\mathbb{F}_q^n\). We also characterize codimension-$k$ cyclically covering subspaces.
  Based on these results, we give a unified characterization of \(h_q(n)\) in the case where $q$ and $n$ are primes with \(n>q\) and $q$ being a primitive root modulo $n$. Specifically, \(h_2(n) \geq 2\) and \(h_q(n) = 0\) for \(q \neq 2\). We prove that \(h_3(n) \ge 1\) for every prime \(n > 3\) with odd \(\operatorname{ord}_n(3)\). Moreover, for any prime \(q > 3\), the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis implies the existence of infinitely many primes \(n > q\) such that $q$ is not a primitive root modulo $n$ and \(h_q(n) = 0\). We provide algebraic interpretations for the inequalities \(h_q(mn)\ge\max\{h_q(m),h_q(n)\}\) and \(h_q(mn)\ge h_q(m)+h_q(n)\). Using Galois descent, we prove \(h_{q^m}(n)\le h_q(n)\). Furthermore, we generalize a class of constructions that achieve the upper bound \(\lfloor\log_q(n)\rfloor\). Finally, under the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, we obtain average lower bounds of \(h_q(n)\) for $q=2,3$.

</details>


### [122] [Some new modular Nahm sums of ranks 3 and 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13590)
*Zhineng Cao,Liuquan Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发现了6个新的模Nahm和族（3个秩3和3个秩4），通过修改Zagier的秩3例子、对Milas和Wang的蝌蚪Nahm和施加升对偶操作以及常数项方法获得，并通过建立Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式证明了其模性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找新的模Nahm和族，扩展模形式与q级数理论。

Method: 修改Zagier的秩3例子；对Milas-Wang的秩3蝌蚪Nahm和施加升对偶操作（得到3个秩4族）；使用常数项方法（得到1个秩4族）；建立Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式将Nahm和表达为无穷乘积以证明模性。

Result: 发现了6个新的模Nahm和族，其中2个秩3族（修改Zagier例子），3个秩4族（升对偶操作），1个秩4族（常数项方法）。

Conclusion: 通过Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式证明了这些Nahm和的模性，并将其表达为模无穷乘积。

Abstract: We discover six new families of modular Nahm sums in ranks 3 and 4. Two of them are rank three sums obtained by modifying two of Zagier's rank three examples. Three rank four families are derived by applying the lift-dual operation to the rank three tadpole Nahm sums studied by Milas and Wang, while the other rank four family is found by the constant term method. To prove modularity, we establish Rogers-Ramanujan type identities that express these Nahm sums as infinite products which are modular.

</details>


### [123] [Split primes and the Elekes-Rónyai problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13619)
*Cosmin Pohoata*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个反例，证明存在绝对常数c>0和任意大的有限实数集A，使得多项式x+y+(x-y)^2在A上的像集大小不超过|A|^{2-c}。由于该多项式既非加法也非乘法，这构成了Elekes-Rónyai问题的一个反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Elekes-Rónyai问题寻找反例，该问题涉及多项式在有限集合上的像集大小与集合大小的关系。

Method: 通过构造特定的多项式x+y+(x-y)^2以及对应的有限实数集A，证明存在绝对常数c>0使得像集大小的上界为|A|^{2-c}。

Result: 存在绝对常数c>0和任意大的有限实数集A，满足|x+y+(x-y)^2的像集| ≤ |A|^{2-c}。

Conclusion: 该多项式x+y+(x-y)^2既非加法也非乘法，但其像集大小远小于|A|^2，因此成为Elekes-Rónyai问题的一个反例。

Abstract: There exist an absolute constant $c>0$ and arbitrarily large finite sets $A\subset \mathbb{R}$ with $$\left| \left\{x+y+(x-y)^2:\ x, y \in A\right\}\right| \le|A|^{2-c}.$$ Since $x+y+(x-y)^2 \in \mathbb{R}[x,y]$ is a polynomial which is neither additive nor multiplicative, this provides a counterexample for the Elekes-Rónyai problem.

</details>

### [124] [Mixed-dimensional quantum Monte Carlo studies of M-point moiré materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12508)
*Dumitru Călugăru,Konstantinos Vasiliou,Haoyu Hu,B. Andrei Bernevig,Werner Krauth,S. A. Parameswaran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对M点扭曲双层SnSe₂材料中出现的混合维度Hubbard模型，开发了一种无符号问题的新型随机级数展开量子蒙特卡洛算法，结合全局更新与并行回火克服平衡化挑战，数值发现了与转角强相关的关联绝缘体相以及Wigner-Mott绝缘体，并用强耦合理论解释了主要数值特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M点扭曲材料（如扭转AA堆叠SnSe₂）是一种新型moire材料平台，其低能电子位于布里渊区三个M点。第一性原理推导的连续模型表明导带电子实现了一种三轨道Hubbard模型，具有轨道选择的准一维（quasi-1D）跳跃，受投影镜面对称性保护。这种“混合维度”极限——每个谷内跳跃为准一维，而谷间通过相互作用耦合为二维网络——在任意填充下均可用SSE QMC无符号问题模拟。然而，混合维度设置给平衡化带来了挑战，需要开发新的高效算法。

Method: 开发了一种结合定制全局更新与并行回火的新型SSE QMC算法；在广泛且实际的转角范围和相互作用强度下，探索了M点扭曲AA堆叠SnSe₂的相图；采用强耦合描述对数值结果进行解析解释。

Result: 在整数填充下，系统支持关联绝缘体，其性质和强度高度依赖于转角；在某些相称分数填充下，发现了Wigner-Mott绝缘体的证据。

Conclusion: 本文的算法和结果不仅揭示了M点扭曲SnSe₂中丰富的关联绝缘体相，而且为其他混合维度Hubbard模型的模拟提供了可推广的方法论框架。

Abstract: A new moiré-material platform has recently been proposed based on twisting two-dimensional triangular-lattice monolayers whose low-energy states lie at the three M points of the Brillouin zone. Continuum models derived from extensive ab initio simulations suggest that electrons in the conduction bands of one such M-point moiré material, twisted AA-stacked SnSe$_2$, realize a three-orbital Hubbard model with orbitally-selective, quasi-one-dimensional (quasi-1D) hopping, protected by a projective mirror symmetry. Here, we show that the resulting "mixed-dimensional" limit -- in which the hopping is exactly quasi-1D in each valley, while the valleys are coupled by interactions into a fully two-dimensional network -- can be sampled with Stochastic Series Expansion (SSE) quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) without a sign problem at any filling. We develop an efficient new SSE QMC algorithm that combines custom global updates with parallel tempering to overcome the equilibration challenges posed by the mixed-dimensional setting. We then use this algorithm to explore the phase diagram of M-point twisted AA-stacked SnSe$_2$. Over extended and realistic ranges of twist angles and interaction strengths, we find that at integer fillings the system supports correlated insulators whose nature and strength depend strongly on angle. At certain commensurate fractional fillings, we further find evidence for Wigner-Mott insulators. We analytically account for the main features observed numerically using a strong-coupling description. Finally, we discuss perturbations away from the mixed-dimensional limit and the possibility of applying our method to other realizations of mixed-dimensional Hubbard models.

</details>


### [125] [Spin correlations, low-energy scales, and anisotropy scaling in kagome frustrated magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12512)
*Phillip Popp,Stephan Rosenkranz,Arthur P. Ramirez,Sergey Syzranov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过XXZ海森堡模型在几何阻挫晶格上的绝热连续性，识别了kagome晶格低温自旋谱函数的普适特征：两个动量无关的峰（ω₁≈3.4T*, ω₂≈6.3T*），其中T*是热容低温峰对应的隐藏能标。低能ω≲T*的谱特征源于单磁子散射，为解释中子散射数据提供了明确的光谱判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋液体的中子散射宽谱特征既可能来自自旋子激发，也可能由无序和强磁子相互作用下的磁子激发产生，现有方法难以区分。本文旨在建立一种新方法，从几何阻挫晶格的绝热连续性出发，识别有限温自旋关联函数的普适特征，为解读中子散射提供清晰判据。

Method: 基于XXZ海森堡模型在几何阻挫晶格上随各向异性参数变化的绝热连续性，分析有限温度自旋关联函数。聚焦kagome晶格，计算低能自旋谱函数，识别动量无关的峰结构及其与隐藏能标T*的关系，并通过单磁子散射解释低能谱特征。

Result: 发现kagome晶格低能自旋谱函数存在两个鲁棒的动量无关峰（ω₁≈3.4T*, ω₂≈6.3T*），T*为热容低温峰对应的能标。低能ω≲T*的谱特征由单磁子散射主导，可确定相应激发的磁化强度。

Conclusion: 本文结果为kagome及其他几何阻挫量子磁体的中子散射光谱解释提供了精确判据，并可推广到其他几何阻挫晶格。

Abstract: Neutron scattering is central to identifying quantum states of magnetic materials. In the search for quantum spin liquids, broad spectral features of inelastic spectra have been cited as evidence for spinon excitations, but can also arise from magnon excitations excitations in the presence of quenched disorder and strong magnon interactions. We develop a new approach to this problem, based on the adiabatic continuity in the $XXZ$ Heisenberg model on geometrically frustrating (GF) lattices as a function of the model's anisotropy. Using this approach, we identify universal features and energies of finite-temperature spin correlators. Focusing on the kagome lattice, we show that the low-energy spin spectral function contains robust, momentum-independent peaks with frequencies: $ω_1 \approx 3.4 T^*$ and $ω_2 \approx 6.3 T^*$, where the ``hidden energy scale'' $T^*$ is the characteristic scale of a low-temperature peak in the heat capacity, at which many GF magnets also display spin-glass freezing. We show that the spectral features at low energies $ω\lesssim T^*$ arise from single-magnon scattering and identify the magnetizations of the respective excitations. We explore the evolution of the spectral features with temperature and discuss extensions to other GF lattices. Our results provide a sharp spectroscopic criterion for interpreting neutron scattering in kagome and other GF quantum magnets.

</details>


### [126] [Cyclotron mass-selective de Haas-van Alphen measurements using temperature modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12514)
*Michelle Hollricher,Andreas Bauer,Leo Maximov,Louw Feenstra,Christian Pfleiderer,Marc A. Wilde*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种温度调制的de Haas-van Alphen测量技术，通过样品温度谐波调制和锁相检测，利用Lifshitz-Kosevich因子的温度导数，选择性激发不同有效质量的量子振荡，成功在MoSi₂中分离出传统方法无法分辨的重轨道贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统de Haas-van Alphen频谱中，重轨道（大有效质量）的弱量子振荡信号往往被轻轨道的强信号掩盖，难以分辨。现有方法无法选择性区分不同有效质量的振荡。

Method: 对样品温度进行谐波调制，利用Lifshitz-Kosevich因子对温度的导数特性，使不同有效质量的量子振荡振幅随温度和磁场呈现非单调演化；结合锁相检测技术，在拾取线圈感应电压中选择性提取特定有效质量的振荡信号。

Result: 在补偿型拓扑半金属MoSi₂中，利用Zeeman效应驱动的谐波成分作为1m*到13m*的天然线性质量梳，实验展示了质量依赖的量子振荡振幅的可调性，成功分离了传统频谱中无法分辨的重轨道弱信号。

Conclusion: 该技术提供了一种可靠的手段，能够选择性隔离不同有效质量的量子振荡，尤其适用于分离被轻轨道强信号掩盖的重轨道贡献，拓展了de Haas-van Alphen效应在复杂电子体系中的应用能力。

Abstract: We present a temperature-modulated de Haas-van Alphen measurement technique that allows selective addressing of quantum oscillations with different effective masses $m^{\ast}$ using a non-monotonic amplitude evolution with temperature and magnetic field, governed by the temperature derivative of the Lifshitz-Kosevich factor. The technique relies on harmonic modulation of the sample temperature and phase-sensitive detection of quantum oscillations in the voltage induced in a pick-up coil. We use a set of frequencies with strong Zeeman-driven harmonic content in the compensated topological semimetal MoSi$_{2}$ as a natural linear mass comb ranging from 1$m^{\ast}$ to 13$m^{\ast}$ to demonstrate the tunability of the mass-dependent quantum oscillation amplitudes experimentally. The technique allows to reliably isolate weak contributions of heavy orbits that are inaccessible in conventional de Haas-van Alphen frequency spectra because their frequency peaks overlap with much stronger frequency peaks of lighter orbits.

</details>


### [127] [Hidden antiferromagnetism, persistent valley fluctuations, and $U(6)$ crossovers in triangular-lattice M-point moiré materials via determinantal quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12530)
*Konstantinos Vasiliou,Dumitru Călugăru,Johannes S. Hofmann,S. A. Parameswaran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于扭转二维原子单层的新型莫尔材料平台，其低能态位于布里渊区的三个M点。通过连续模型和从头算模拟，发现导带电子实现了具有谷选择、准一维跳跃的三谷Hubbard模型，且同位库仑排斥近似具有U(6)对称性。作者展示了该系统在每莫尔原胞三个电子填充下可进行无符号问题的行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟，并探索了不同相互作用强度和U(6)破缺各向异性下的相图。对于近各向同性相互作用（如AA堆叠扭转SnSe₂），系统表现出局域矩形成与巡游性竞争的扩展中等耦合区域，其向低温有序态的渡越可由涨落的U(6)局域矩理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出基于扭转二维原子单层的莫尔材料新平台，其低能态位于布里渊区M点；研究该体系中三谷Hubbard模型的强关联物理，特别是U(6)对称性下的相互作用效应。

Method: 采用连续模型和从头算模拟建立三谷Hubbard模型，利用无符号问题的行列式量子蒙特卡洛方法模拟每莫尔原胞三个电子填充下的系统行为，分析不同相互作用强度和U(6)破缺各向异性下的相图。

Result: 发现近各向同性相互作用下系统存在扩展的中等耦合区域，其中局域矩形成与巡游性相互竞争；低温有序态的渡越可由涨落的U(6)局域矩理解；这些特征在理想的无符号问题极限之外仍然存在。

Conclusion: 该莫尔材料平台为研究具有U(6)对称性的强关联电子系统提供了新途径，无符号量子蒙特卡洛模拟揭示了丰富的强关联物理行为，且这些现象具有一定普适性。

Abstract: A new moiré material platform was recently proposed based on twisting two-dimensional atomic monolayers whose low-energy states lie at the three M-points of the Brillouin Zone. Continuum and ab initio modeling suggest that electrons in the conduction bands of these materials realize three-valley Hubbard models with valley-selective, quasi-one-dimensional hopping. Remarkably, the onsite Hubbard repulsion is almost $U(6)$-symmetric without fine-tuning. Here, we show that this class of systems naturally admits sign-free determinantal Quantum Monte Carlo simulations at a filling of three electrons per moiré unit cell. We use these to explore the phase diagram for interactions of various strengths and $U(6)$-breaking anisotropies. We show that for near-isotropic interactions as relevant to, e.g., AA-stacked twisted SnSe$_2$, the system exhibits an extended intermediate-coupling regime in which local-moment formation and itinerancy compete, and the crossover to a putative low-temperature ordered state can be understood in terms of fluctuating $U(6)$ local moments. We argue that many of these features persist beyond the idealized sign-problem-free limit.

</details>


### [128] [Hall conductivity reveals the nature of quantum coherence in strongly correlated metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12665)
*Emily Z. Zhang,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过Hubbard模型的精确量子蒙特卡洛模拟，本文发现虽然纵向电阻率普遍呈现T-线性行为（奇异金属的特征），但霍尔响应对粒子-空穴不对称性、费米面拓扑和多体关联效应高度敏感，能够揭示从半经典到量子相干输运的交叉，而这一交叉在纵向通道中被掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属中纵向输运的T-线性电阻率已被广泛研究，但横向（霍尔）响应中包含的微观信息往往被忽视，作者希望利用霍尔响应揭示纵向通道中无法直接观测的量子相干输运交叉行为。

Method: 采用精确的determinantal量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）模拟，对掺杂Hubbard模型在磁场下的纵向和横向输运进行计算。

Result: 电阻率在不同参数下均呈现稳健的T-线性行为；霍尔响应对粒子-空穴不对称性、费米面拓扑和多体关联效应高度敏感；这些效应共同决定了系统进入量子相干态的交叉能标，并体现在霍尔电导率中。

Conclusion: 纵向电阻率的T-线性行为具有普遍性，但霍尔响应能揭示从半经典到量子相干输运的交叉，这一交叉在纵向通道中无法被观察到。

Abstract: Linear-in-temperature resistivity is a hallmark for strange metallic transport, and appears universally in many strongly correlated electron systems. However, the focus on the longitudinal channel often overshadows the profound microscopic insights contained within the transverse response. Here, we utilize numerically exact determinantal quantum Monte Carlo simulations of the doped Hubbard model in a magnetic field to calculate longitudinal and transverse transport. We demonstrate that while the resistivity is robustly $T$-linear across parameter sets, the Hall response is highly sensitive to particle-hole asymmetry, Fermi surface topology, and many-body correlation effects. Specifically, the combination of these effects determine a crossover scale in which the system becomes quantum-coherent, and is reflected in the Hall conductivity. Our results demonstrate that while the $T$-linearity in resistivity appears universal, the Hall response reveals a crossover from semi-classical to quantum-coherent transport otherwise masked in the longitudinal channel.

</details>


### [129] [Selective stabilization of antiferromagnetic orders in FeTe films via local strain engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13257)
*Hao Xu,Jing Jiang,Xuesong Gai,Haicheng Lin,Kai Liu,Zhong-Yi Lu,Kai Chang,Chong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合高分辨率扫描隧道显微镜和密度泛函理论计算，在少层FeTe薄膜中利用局部单轴应变工程实现了双共线型和二聚体型反铁磁序的选择性稳定。研究发现沿Fe-Fe次近邻方向的单轴压缩稳定双共线反铁磁序，沿Fe-Fe最近邻方向的各向异性应变实现了长程二聚体反铁磁序，其表现为√2×√2的电子重构。该工作提供了操控竞争磁性序的稳健策略，揭示了铁基超导体中晶格、自旋和电子自由度之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FeTe母体化合物具有复杂的磁性景观且对晶格畸变高度敏感，理论模型预测拉伸应变下存在双共线到二聚体反铁磁相变，但由于严重的磁阻挫，其实验实现和确定性调控一直难以实现。本研究旨在通过应变工程实现对这些竞争磁性序的选择性稳定和调控。

Method: 结合高分辨率扫描隧道显微镜(STM)和密度泛函理论(DFT)计算，在少层FeTe薄膜中通过局部单轴应变工程，映射位错区域附近的应变场并建立特定应变分量与磁性基态的直接对应关系。

Result: 1) 沿Fe-Fe次近邻方向的单轴压缩稳定双共线反铁磁序，条纹取向平行于压缩轴；2) 首次实验实现了长程二聚体反铁磁序，在沿Fe-Fe最近邻方向的各向异性应变下出现，表现为√2×√2电子重构；3) 二聚体相与双共线相具有相同的奈尔温度。

Conclusion: 各向异性应变有效消除了竞争态之间的磁性简并，为操控 elusive 磁性序提供了稳健策略，并为理解铁基超导体中晶格、自旋和电子自由度之间的相互作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: The parent compound FeTe hosts a complex magnetic landscape that is highly susceptible to lattice distortions. Although theoretical models have predicted a bicollinear to dimer antiferromagnetic (AFM) phase transition under tensile strain, its experimental realization and deterministic control has remained elusive owing to severe magnetic frustration. Here, combining high-resolution scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and density functional theory (DFT) calculations, we demonstrate the selective stabilization of bicollinear and dimer AFM orders in few-layer FeTe films via local uniaxial strain engineering. By mapping the strain fields near dislocation areas in FeTe films and FeTe/FeSe heterostructures, we establish a direct correspondence between specific strain components and the resulting magnetic ground states. We find that uniaxial compression along the Fe-Fe next-nearest-neighbor direction stabilizes the bicollinear AFM order, with the stripe orientation aligning parallel to the compression axis. Crucially, we report the experimental realization of the long-range dimer AFM order, which emerges under anisotropic strain along the Fe-Fe nearest-neighbor direction. This phase manifests as a distinct $\sqrt{2} \times \sqrt{2}$ electronic reconstruction and shares a common Neel temperature with the bicollinear phase. Our findings reveal that anisotropic strain effectively lifts the magnetic degeneracy among competing states. This work provides a robust strategy for the manipulation of elusive magnetic orders and offers insights into the interplay between lattice, spin, and electronic degrees of freedom in iron-based superconductors.

</details>


### [130] [Phase diagram of the Kitaev-Heisenberg-$Γ$ model: Classical and quantum magnetism, frustration, and subdominant interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13263)
*Kiyu Fukui,Yukitoshi Motome*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用最先进的数值技术，从经典和量子两种视角重新研究了Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ模型的基态相图。经典情形下揭示了多种非共线多-Q磁序（含非公度调制），量子情形下量子涨落抑制了竞争序，减少了主导的非公度序。进一步识别出高度阻挫区域，其中螺旋自旋液态和新的磁有序态可能被其他相互作用稳定。结果为Kitaev自旋液体候选材料的实验解释和新材料设计提供了全面指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev自旋液体是精确可解的量子自旋液体态，但实际候选材料中Kitaev相互作用不可避免地伴随Heisenberg和Γ等附加磁相互作用，这些相互作用常在低温下诱导磁有序，因此厘清其效应对于寻找和设计Kitaev自旋液体候选材料至关重要。

Method: 采用最先进的数值技术，从经典和量子两种视角重新审视Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ模型的基态相图。经典情形下分析各种非共线多-Q磁序，量子情形下考察量子涨落对竞争序的抑制效应。

Result: 经典情形下发现多种非共线多-Q磁序（含非公度调制）；量子情形下量子涨落抑制了竞争序，减少了主导的非公度序；识别出高度阻挫区域，其中螺旋自旋液态和新的磁有序态可能被其他相互作用稳定。

Conclusion: 为Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ模型提供了经典和量子自旋的全面视角，为实验解释候选材料以及寻找和设计新的Kitaev自旋液体材料提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: The Kitaev spin liquid provides a rare example of exactly solvable quantum spin liquid states. Intensive research over the past two decades has identified a variety of its candidate materials. In real materials, however, the Kitaev interaction is inevitably accompanied by additional magnetic interactions such as the Heisenberg and $Γ$ interactions. These interactions often induce magnetic ordering at low temperatures, making it essential to clarify their effects in the search for and design of Kitaev spin liquid candidate materials. In this study, we revisit the ground-state phase diagram of the Kitaev-Heisenberg-$Γ$ model from both classical and quantum perspectives, using state-of-the-art numerical techniques. In the classical case, we reveal a $zoo$ $of$ $noncollinear$ $orders$, where a variety of noncollinear multiple-$Q$ magnetic orders with and without incommensurate modulations emerge. In the quantum case, we unravel that quantum fluctuations suppress many of the competing orders found in the classical case, resulting in a reduced number of dominant incommensurate orders. We further identify $highly$ $frustrated$ regions, where spiral spin liquid states as well as new magnetically ordered states are potentially stabilized by other additional magnetic interactions. Our results provide a comprehensive perspective on the Kitaev-Heisenberg-$Γ$ model for both classical and quantum spins and offer a valuable guide not only for interpreting experimental results on candidate materials, but also for searching and designing new materials to realize the Kitaev spin liquid.

</details>


### [131] [Frustration effects on the magnetization plateau physics in a trimerized quantum spin-1/2 chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13265)
*L. M. Ramos,M. Schmidt,F. M. Zimmer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过团簇平均场与Lanczos精确对角化方法，研究了三聚化量子自旋链中阻挫诱导的不稳定性，揭示了弱阻挫下的1/3磁化平台、强阻挫下的双子关联交叉行为，以及零场临界性，为理解三聚化自旋系统中的分数化激发提供了微观框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Na₂Cu₃Ge₄O₁₂化合物的最新实验发现启发，探究三聚化量子自旋链中阻挫诱导的不稳定性及其导致的基态和量子信息性质变化。

Method: 采用团簇平均场方法结合Lanczos精确对角化，分析具有竞争相互作用的Heisenberg模型在外磁场中的基态和量子信息性质，并进行能量间隙、von Neumann纠缠熵和保真度敏感度的有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 弱阻挫区域系统呈现稳健的1/3磁化平台，对应集体亚铁磁三聚体态；增大次近邻三聚体内耦合导致自旋关联重组，过渡到双子关联区域；通过有限尺寸标度表征了零场临界性。

Conclusion: 阻挫能导致性质不同的量子态，为理解三聚化量子自旋系统中分数化激发的出现提供了微观框架。

Abstract: We investigate frustration-induced instabilities in a trimerized quantum spin chain motivated by recent experimental findings for the compound Na$_2$Cu$_3$Ge$_4$O$_{12}$. Employing a cluster mean-field approach combined with Lanczos exact diagonalization, we analyze the ground-state and quantum-information properties of a Heisenberg model with competing interactions in a magnetic field. In the weakly frustrated regime, the system exhibits a robust $1/3$ magnetization plateau associated with a collective ferrimagnetic-like trimer state. Increasing the next-nearest-neighbor intratrimer coupling drives a pronounced reorganization of spin correlations, leading to a crossover toward a doublon-like correlation regime and providing a static ground-state picture consistent with the composite excitations observed dynamically in trimerized chains. The resulting low-energy behavior can be interpreted in terms of weakly interacting emergent spins, offering a microscopic explanation for the extended stability of the magnetization plateau. Furthermore, through finite-size scaling analyses of the energy gap, von Neumann entanglement entropy, and fidelity susceptibility, we characterize the zero-field criticality of the model. Ultimately, our results suggest that frustration gives rise to qualitatively distinct quantum states and provide a microscopic framework for understanding the emergence of fractionalized excitations in trimerized quantum spin systems.

</details>


### [132] [Modeling strained Cd$_3$As$_2$ thin films and their behavior in magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13293)
*M. Smith,A. A. Burkov,P. P. Orth,I. Martin,Victor L. Quito*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Cd₃As₂薄膜在不同应变类型（双轴/单轴）和磁场下的电子行为，构建了k·p有效模型，揭示了应变对称性与磁输运性质之间的关系，并提出了区分不同微观机制的实验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Cd₃As₂薄膜在应变和磁场下的电子结构变化，解释Ahadi等人(2025)实验中的反常磁输运现象，并建立应变-对称性-能带结构之间的系统关联。

Method: 基于对称性分析构建k·p有效模型，分别考虑双轴应变（4mm点群，保留C₄旋转对称但破缺反演）和单轴应变（保留反演的mmm点群/破缺反演的2mm点群）两种情况；结合垂直磁场和外加面内磁场的效应，分析亚带结构和Landau能级行为。

Result: (1) 垂直磁场下出现两种可能的微观机制可解释实验现象；(2) 提出了区分这两种机制的新方法；(3) 最低Landau能级打开能隙需要对称性降低至2mm（同时破缺C₄旋转和反演）或应变诱导能带重整化导致的拓扑相变；(4) 足够大的面内磁场可诱导二维狄拉克半金属相，该相高度依赖于应变轮廓，带接触发生在磁场垂直于保留镜面方向时。

Conclusion: 应变对称性是调控Cd₃As₂薄膜拓扑电子态的关键参数；面内磁场可作为探测材料应变轮廓的有效探针。

Abstract: We present a systematic analysis of the behavior of thin films of Cd$_3$As$_2$ under different strain profiles and in magnetic fields. In each case, we construct effective $k \cdot p$ models by considering the reduction of symmetry and all constraints imposed by the remaining symmetries. Our analysis naturally describes both in-plane biaxial and uniaxial strain. Biaxial strain is expected to preserve in-plane $C_4$ rotational symmetry while breaking inversion, allowing for a description in terms of the $4mm$ point group. Uniaxial strain, on the other hand, breaks $C_4$ symmetry. For this case, we consider two scenarios: one preserving inversion, described by the $mmm$ group, and one breaking it, leading to $2mm$ symmetry. After deriving the models, we examine the effects of out-of-plane magnetic fields, identifying two possible microscopic mechanisms that can account for the experimental results reported in Ahadi et al. (2025). Importantly, our analysis proposes a new method for differentiating between them. By incorporating the effects of multiple subbands along the confinement direction, we show that the opening of a gap in the lowest Landau level requires either reducing the symmetry down to $2mm$, breaking both inversion and $C_4$ rotations, or a topological transition of the band structure due to strain-induced band renormalization. Furthermore, we demonstrate that a two-dimensional Dirac semimetal phase can be induced by sufficiently large in-plane magnetic fields. This phase is highly sensitive to different strain profiles, with band touchings occurring when the field is applied perpendicular to preserved mirror planes, serving as a powerful probe of the material's strain profile.

</details>


### [133] [Variational Monte Carlo study of a two-orbital Hubbard model for the iron pnictides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13342)
*Vito Marino,Gabriele Gatti,Massimo Capone,Luca F. Tocchio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过变分蒙特卡洛方法研究两轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型，发现超导区域仅在半满系统处于Mott态时出现，且无轨道选择性，结果与三轨道模型不同，更类似单带Hubbard模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究两轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型中的超导配对机制及其与轨道选择性的关系，与三轨道模型及单带Hubbard模型进行对比。

Method: 采用变分蒙特卡洛方法，对两轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型在空穴掺杂和电子掺杂（相对于半满n=2）的非磁性区域进行数值模拟。

Result: 仅在系统处于Mott态时观察到s±对称性的超导区域，且无轨道选择性出现。

Conclusion: 两轨道模型的超导行为与三轨道模型有本质差异——后者超导区域更广且伴随轨道选择性，而前者结果更接近单带Hubbard模型的特征。

Abstract: We study a two-orbital Hubbard-Kanamori model, which has been originally proposed for iron-based superconductors, using variational Monte Carlo. We span the nonmagnetic sector at both hole-doping and electron-doping, with respect to the half-filled case $n=2$. We report the presence of a superconductive region with a $s^{\pm}$ symmetry only when the half-filled system is in a Mott state, while orbital selectivity is absent. These results are qualitatively different from what was reported in the three-orbital Hubbard-Kanamori model, where a more extended superconductive region was observed with a concomitant development of orbital selectivity, and they are to some extent more reminiscent of the single-band Hubbard model.

</details>


### [134] [Overview of the Theory of Extremely Correlated Fermi Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13541)
*B Sriram Shastry*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了极相关费米液体（ECFL）理论作为理解接近Mott绝缘体极限的金属体系中t-J模型的理论框架，并将理论结果与单层高温超导体系的电阻率和谱学数据进行了比较，发现密度依赖的准线性温度依赖电阻、异常小的准粒子权重以及低温涌现能标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解接近Mott绝缘体极限的金属体系中t-J模型提供ECFL理论框架，并以非专家可读的形式呈现，同时通过实验数据验证理论预测。

Method: 回顾极相关费米液体（ECFL）理论的基本思想和方程，将理论结果与单层高温超导体系的电阻率数据和部分谱学数据进行对比分析。

Result: 发现了密度依赖的准线性温度依赖电阻、异常小的准粒子权重，以及控制单层高温超导体系输运、热力学和谱学性质的独特低温涌现能标。

Conclusion: ECFL理论能够较好地描述接近Mott绝缘体的强关联金属体系，并对进一步的实验探测提出了建议。

Abstract: The Extremely Correlated Fermi Liquids (ECFL) theory is reviewed as a framework for understanding the $t$-$J$ model in metallic systems close to the Mott insulating limit. This overview presents the underlying ideas and the resulting equations in a form accessible to nonexperts. We compare theoretical results with all available resistivity data for single-layer High-T$_{c}$ systems, and with some spectral data. The highlighted results include a density dependent quasilinear T-dependence in resistivity, an unusually small quasiparticle weight, and distinct low-temperature emergent scales that dominate transport, thermodynamics and spectral properties of single-layer High T$_c$ systems. Suggestions are made for further experiments to probe the physics of these challenging quantum many-body systems.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII探测器在3000.00-3119.88 MeV能量范围内采集的e+e-湮灭数据，研究了e+e-→KS0K+π-+c.c.的截面线形，测量了J/ψ强衰变与电磁衰变振幅的相对相位和大小，并通过分波分析获得了子过程的截面和分支比，观测到相对相位偏离正交关系超过3σ，未发现直接CP破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究e+e-→KS0K+π-+c.c.过程的截面线形，测量J/ψ强衰变与电磁衰变振幅的相对相位和大小，检验振幅纯实数的假设和正交性关系，并精确测量相关分支比。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的26个质心能量点（3000.00-3119.88 MeV）的e+e-湮灭数据（总积分亮度约440.7 pb-1），通过分波分析（Partial Wave Analysis）研究截面线形，提取相对相位和振幅大小。

Result: J/ψ强衰变与电磁衰变振幅的相对相位和大小为(123.7±5.3)°;4.31±0.22 或 (-123.1±5.2)°;4.38±0.22。分支比B(J/ψ→KS0K+π-+c.c.)=(5.17±0.20)或(5.36±0.20)×10-3。子过程相对相位均偏离正交关系超过3σ，分支比精度提高两倍以上。未发现直接CP破坏。

Conclusion: 测量的相对相位与振幅纯实数假设及正交性预期存在超过3σ的显著偏离；分支比与世界平均值一致但精度提高两倍以上；电磁贡献扣除后的强振幅比偏离单位1约2.1-2.3σ；未观察到直接CP破坏证据。

Abstract: Using $e^+e^-$ annihilation data collected at 26 center-of-mass energy points between 3000.00 and $3119.88~\text{MeV}$ with the BESIII detector, corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of about $440.7~\text{pb}^{-1}$, we study the cross section lineshape of $e^+e^-\to K_S^0 K^+π^-+c.c.$. The relative phases and magnitudes between $J/ψ$ strong and electromagnetic decay amplitudes are measured to be $(123.7\pm5.3)^\circ;4.31\pm0.22$ or $(-123.1\pm5.2)^\circ;4.38\pm0.22$, with corresponding branching fractions $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to K_S^0 K^+π^-+c.c.)=(5.17\pm0.20)$ or $(5.36\pm0.20)\times10^{-3}$. Based on a partial wave analysis, the cross sections of $e^+e^-\to\bar K^0 K^*(892)^0+c.c.$ and $e^+e^-\to K^+ K^*(892)^-+c.c.$ are obtained. For these subprocesses, the relative phases and magnitudes are determined as $(155.2\pm15.5)^{\circ};3.67\pm0.27$ or $(-154.1\pm15.5)^{\circ};3.71\pm0.25$ and $(180.1\pm31.8)^{\circ};25.06\pm2.51$, respectively. The large relative phases deviate from the orthogonality relation expected from experiment and from the assumption of purely real amplitudes by more than $3σ$. The measured branching fractions $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to\bar K^0 K^*(892)^0)+c.c.=(4.18\pm0.18)$ or $(4.31\pm0.19)\times10^{-3}$, $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to K^+ K^*(892)^-+c.c.)=(7.09\pm0.28)\times10^{-3}$ are all consistent with the world average values, but achieve better than a twofold improvement in precision. The ratios between the branching fractions of $J/ψ\to\bar K^0 K^*(892)^0+c.c.$ and $J/ψ\to\bar K^+ K^*(892)^-+c.c.$ are $\mathcal{R}_{K^*\bar{K}}=0.589\pm0.012$ or $0.612\pm0.013$. After subtracting the electromagnetic contribution, the corresponding strong amplitude ratios are $\mathcal{R}^{3g}_{K^*\bar{K}}=0.884\pm0.050$ or $0.909\pm0.044$, which deviate $2.3$ or $2.1σ$ from the unity. No evidence for direct $CP$ violation is observed.

</details>

Yin,K. Yoshihara,C. Z. Yuan,J. Yuan,L. Yuan,Y. Yusa,L. Zani,F. Zeng,M. Zeyrek,B. Zhang,X. Zhao,V. Zhilich,J. S. Zhou,Q. D. Zhou,L. Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次观测到B+ → Σ_c(2455)++ Ξ̄_c'⁻ 和 B⁰ → Σ_c(2455)⁰ Ξ̄_c'⁰ 衰变过程，显著性分别达到6.4σ和5.3σ。基于Belle和Belle II实验数据，测量了分支比，这是首次观测到B介子衰变到属于同一SU(3)味道六重态的一对粲重子-反重子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测B介子衰变到一对属于同一SU(3)味道六重态的粲重子-反重子态，填补该衰变通道的实验空白。

Method: 基于Belle探测器在KEKB对撞机收集的771.6×10⁶个Υ(4S)衰变样本和Belle II探测器在SuperKEKB对撞机收集的520.6×10⁶个Υ(4S)衰变样本进行数据分析。

Result: B⁺ → Σ_c(2455)⁺⁺ Ξ̄_c'⁻ 和 B⁰ → Σ_c(2455)⁰ Ξ̄_c'⁰ 的显著性分别为6.4σ和5.3σ；分支比测量结果分别为(1.68±0.31±0.12^{+1.49}_{-0.54})×10⁻³ 和 (1.28±0.32±0.10^{+0.30}_{-0.21})×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 首次观测到B介子衰变到属于同一SU(3)味道六重态的粲重子-反重子对，为理解强相互作用和重子产生机制提供了新的实验依据。

Abstract: We report the first observation of the decays $B^{+} \to Σ_{c}(2455)^{++} \barΞ_{c}^{\prime-}$ and $B^{0} \to Σ_{c}(2455)^{0} \barΞ_{c}^{\prime0}$, with significances of $6.4\,σ$ and $5.3\, σ$, respectively, including systematic uncertainties. This analysis is based on data samples containing $771.6 \times 10^{6}$ $Υ(4S)$ decays collected with the Belle detector at the KEKB collider and $520.6 \times 10^{6}$ $Υ(4S)$ decays collected with the Belle~II detector at the SuperKEKB collider. The branching fractions are measured to be $\mathcal{B}(B^+ \to Σ_c(2455)^{++} \barΞ_c^{\prime -}) = (1.68 \pm 0.31 \pm 0.12^{+1.49}_{-0.54}) \times 10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(B^0 \to Σ_c(2455)^{0} \barΞ_c^{\prime 0}) = (1.28 \pm 0.32 \pm 0.10^{+0.30}_{-0.21}) \times 10^{-3}$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively, and the third arises from the uncertainties in the absolute branching fractions of $\barΞ_{c}^{-}$ and $\barΞ_{c}^{0}$ decays. This result represents the first observation of $B$-meson decays into a pair of charmed baryon-antibaryon states belonging to the same $SU(3)$ flavor sextet.

</details>


### [137] [Optimization of muon suppression using sweeper magnets for the Forward Physics Facility at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13062)
*Akitaka Ariga,Tomoko Ariga,Jeremy Atkinson,Jamie Boyd,Kohei Chinone,Radu Dobre,Elena Firu,Haruhi Fujimori,Stephen Gibson,Daiki Hayakawa,Enrique Kajomovitz,Alex Keyken,Umut Kose,Laurie Nevay,Ken Ohashi,Simon Thor*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用扫场磁铁抑制高亮度LHC前方物理设施（FPF）中TeV中微子探测器的前向μ子本底。通过SIBYLL事件生成、BDSIM束流传输、Geant4粒子跟踪和真实磁场图的模拟框架，优化了多级磁铁系统配置。无磁铁时μ子通量为3.8×10³ cm⁻²/fb⁻¹，仅LHC隧道内一磁铁即降至2×10³ cm⁻²/fb⁻¹，优化配置下TI18隧道和FPF入口附加磁铁进一步降至1.5×10³ cm⁻²/fb⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度LHC的前向物理设施（FPF）虽能实现TeV中微子高统计测量，但远前向区域的中微子探测器面临强前向μ子本底挑战，需要有效抑制策略。

Method: 采用组合模拟框架：SIBYLL进行事件生成、BDSIM模拟束流传输、Geant4追踪粒子、使用真实磁场图；在LHC隧道、TI18隧道和FPF入口三级位置优化配置扫场磁铁。

Result: 无磁铁时μ子通量3.8×10³ cm⁻²/fb⁻¹；单个LHC隧道磁铁达目标水平2×10³ cm⁻²/fb⁻¹；优化多级配置（TI18+FPF入口附加磁铁）降至1.5×10³ cm⁻²/fb⁻¹。

Conclusion: 优化设计的多级扫场磁铁系统能显著降低前向μ子本底，同时强调真实传输模拟和几何约束对进一步抑制的重要性。

Abstract: The Forward Physics Facility (FPF) at the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) will enable high-statistics measurements of TeV-scale neutrinos, but the intense flux of forward muons poses a major challenge for neutrino detectors in the far-forward region.
  We investigate the suppression of background muons using sweeper magnets with a simulation framework combining SIBYLL event generation, BDSIM beam transport, Geant4 particle tracking, and realistic magnetic field maps.
  Starting from a muon flux of $3.8\times10^3~\mathrm{cm^{-2}}$ per $\mathrm{fb^{-1}}$ without magnets, a magnet in the LHC tunnel alone achieves the target level of $2\times10^3~\mathrm{cm^{-2}}$ per $\mathrm{fb^{-1}}$. Additional magnets at the TI18 tunnel and FPF entrance further reduce the flux to $1.5\times10^3~\mathrm{cm^{-2}}$ per $\mathrm{fb^{-1}}$ in the optimized configuration.
  These results demonstrate that a properly optimized multi-stage sweeper magnet system can significantly reduce the forward muon background, while also highlighting the importance of realistic transport simulations and geometrical constraints in achieving further suppression.

</details>


### [138] [AgentRivet: an automated system for producing Rivet routines from journal publications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13535)
*Antonio J. Costa,Caterina Doglioni,Christian Gütschow,Andrew D. Pilkington,Sukanya Sinha*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大型语言模型的自动化工作流AgentRivet，用于从已发表论文中提取物理分析信息并生成缺失的Rivet例程，填补了粒子物理实验中仅39%测量有Rivet例程的空白。测试表明该方案能生成语法错误少、物理保真度合理的例程，但物理实现问题仍存在，主要源于论文中模糊的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理对撞实验的分析保存策略中，Rivet例程的覆盖率仅为39%，大量测量缺乏公开可用的Rivet例程，阻碍了新理论模型与实验数据的对比以及蒙特卡洛事件生成器的开发和调优。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于大型语言模型（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google的商业模型）的多步骤自动化工作流AgentRivet，包含从论文中提取物理分析信息、编写缺失的Rivet例程，并在中间步骤引入代码审查和物理审查作为自主质量控制机制。

Result: 针对ATLAS和CMS两个近期测量，AgentRivet能够生成语法错误较少的Rivet例程，物理保真度合理且与发表论文的解释一致。多数物理实现问题源于论文中微妙但模糊的定义，部分模型在实现复杂可观测值时即使定义清晰也存在困难。

Conclusion: AgentRivet证明了LLM自动化生成Rivet例程的可行性，但物理实现问题仍需关注，尤其是论文中定义模糊导致的实现偏差。该工作为提高分析覆盖率提供了新的自动化途径。

Abstract: Particle physics collider experiments provide Rivet routines as part of the analysis preservation strategy for model-independent measurements. Rivet is a C++ toolkit that allow new theoretical models to be compared to the measurements, thus aiding the development and tuning of Monte Carlo event generators as well as searches for physics beyond the Standard Model. However, analysis coverage is known to be incomplete, with only 39% of measurements having documented and publicly available Rivet routines. In this article, we design and implement an automated workflow based on Large Language Models with the goal of providing the missing routines. This multi-step workflow, referred to as AgentRivet, extracts the physics analysis information from published papers and writes the missing Rivet routines, with intermediate code- and physics- reviews as part of an autonomous quality control. We report the results obtained using commercial Large Language Models, provided by OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google, for two recent measurements from the ATLAS and CMS experiments. We find that AgentRivet produces competent Rivet routines with few syntax errors. The physics fidelity of the routines is reasonable and follows the explanations given in the relevant publications. Nevertheless, physics-implementation issues do arise and are investigated using the artefacts produced by AgentRivet. The majority of physics implementation issues arise from subtle-but-ambiguous definitions in the given publication, although some models struggle to implement complex observables even when clear definitions are given.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: MicroBooNE实验利用液态氩时间投影室首次在亚GeV中微子能量下测量了氩核上的带电电流相干π产生截面，通量平均截面为(9.1±1.2统计±1.2系统)×10⁻⁴⁰ cm²/Ar，该过程具有干净的两体运动学特征，可用于约束中微子振荡实验中的通量不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量氩核上带电电流相干π产生的截面，填补亚GeV中微子能量下该过程在氩靶上的实验空白，并为DUNE等振荡实验提供通量约束工具。

Method: 利用MicroBooNE液态氩时间投影室，暴露于费米实验室Booster中微子束流，采集对应1.26×10²¹质子打靶的数据，平均中微子能量0.8 GeV，通过干净的两体运动学末态（前向μ子和π介子，无核碎裂）进行事件筛选与截面提取。

Result: 通量平均截面为(9.1±1.2统计±1.2系统)×10⁻⁴⁰ cm²/Ar，这是亚GeV中微子能量下氩核上带电电流相干π产生的首次测量。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了氩核上相干π产生截面的实验空白，其干净的两体运动学特征可用于约束中微子通量不确定性，对DUNE等未来振荡实验具有重要意义。

Abstract: We report a measurement of the charged-current coherent pion production cross section on argon using the MicroBooNE liquid argon time projection chamber exposed to the Booster Neutrino Beam at Fermilab. The measurement uses the MicroBooNE data set corresponding to $1.26 \times 10^{21}$ protons on target with a mean neutrino energy of $0.8$~GeV. The flux-averaged cross section is measured to be $(9.1 \pm 1.2_{\text{stat}} \pm 1.2_\text{syst}) \times 10^{-40}\,\text{cm}^2/\text{Ar}$. This result represents the first measurement of charged-current coherent pion production on argon at sub-GeV neutrino energies. Due to its clean two-body kinematics, where the neutrino interacts coherently with the entire nucleus producing a forward muon and pion with no nuclear breakup, this process provides a useful tool for constraining neutrino flux uncertainties in current and future oscillation experiments such as DUNE.

</details>
